characteristics. Depending on the country of the hotel, these prices may be either: pre-tax, including VAT only, or inclusive of all taxes (VAT and tourist tax included). When it is not stipulated that all taxes are included on a price, taxes (VAT and/or tourist tax) shall be indicated in the following steps of the booking process. â€‹</p></div><div data-testid="legalnotices-text-paragraph" class="legal-notices-block__legal-notice body-default-02"><p>All bookings, wherever they are made, are payable in the hotel's currency.â€‹ Only the amount confirmed during the booking in the currency of the hotel is guaranteed. Conversion to the customer's currency is given for reference only and is not part of the contract. Costs linked to conversions between the hotel's currency and that of the customer (exchange rates, bank fees) shall be paid by the customer.â€‹</p></div><div data-testid="legalnotices-text-paragraph" class="legal-notices-block__legal-notice body-default-02"><p>The amount is converted according to the exchange rate of the day provided by our partner DEVISEA, with the Euro as reference currency.</p></div><div data-testid="legalnotices-text-paragraph" class="legal-notices-block__legal-notice body-default-02"><p>The promotion displayed applies to the standard rate of the day offered by the hotel.
